We are delighted to announce that Camping D.F.E. is officially open for the season starting  May 1st, 2024 ! Whether you’re a returning guest or planning your first visit, our campground offers a perfect blend of natural beauty and recreational activities for all ages.

Who We Are
Discover Nature’s Wonders at Camping D.F.E.
At  Camping D.F.E , we offer an array of attractions and activities designed to create unforgettable experiences for visitors of all ages. Our magical cascading waterfall is a must-see, providing a serene and picturesque backdrop to your outdoor adventures. Enjoy a round of golf on our scenic 9-hole Par 3 course, which boasts beautiful views of the lake and waterfall. Dive into fun at our semi-Olympic swimming pool or explore our two stunning lakes, perfect for fishing, kayaking, and relaxation. Whether you’re here for adventure or tranquility,  Camping D.F.E  has something for everyone.

Qui Nous Sommes
Découvrez les merveilles de la nature au Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée
Au  Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée , nous offrons une variété d’attractions et d’activités conçues pour créer des expériences inoubliables pour les visiteurs de tous âges. Notre cascade magique est un incontournable, offrant un décor serein et pittoresque à vos aventures en plein air. Profitez d’une partie de golf sur notre magnifique parcours de 9 trous Par 3, qui offre de belles vues sur le lac et la cascade. Plongez dans le plaisir dans notre piscine semi-olympique ou explorez nos deux magnifiques lacs, parfaits pour la pêche, le kayak et la détente. Que vous soyez ici pour l’aventure ou la tranquillité, le  Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée  a quelque chose pour tout le monde.

Why You Should Camp With Us
https://www.campingfrontiere.ca/why-you-should-camp-with-us/
https://www.campingfrontiere.ca/why-you-should-camp-with-us/#respond
Fri, 13 Jun 2025 13:41:01 +0000
https://campingfrontiere.ca/?p=3023
If you’re looking for a camping experience that blends nature, comfort, and a sense of freedom, you’ve just found your perfect destination. At Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée, we believe that camping should be more than just pitching a tent—it should be a chance to escape the noise, reconnect with what matters, and create lasting memories with the people (and pets) you love.
Here’s why campers from all over choose to spend their time with us—and why you should too.
A Campground That Feels Like a National Park
Our campground spans over 200 acres of unspoiled natural beauty—a size that rivals some small national parks. This isn’t your typical cramped campground where sites are lined up side by side. Here, nature is the star of the show. You’ll find yourself surrounded by towering trees, winding trails, open meadows, and tranquil water features. Whether you prefer a quiet walk through the forest or lounging by the lake, there’s a sense of peace here that’s hard to find elsewhere.
And because of our size, even on the busiest weekends, there’s still plenty of space to explore without ever feeling crowded. It’s ideal for both relaxation and adventure.
Paddle on Our Peaceful Lake
Nothing says “summer escape” like time on the water—and our on-site lake is perfect for paddle boating. Glide across calm waters surrounded by trees and birdsong, or simply enjoy the view from the shore. It’s a favorite among families, couples, and solo travelers who just want a moment of calm in nature.
Natural Waterfall and Streams
Few campgrounds can offer the soothing sights and sounds of a natural waterfall, but we’re proud to say we can. Our grounds are home to a lovely waterfall and two fresh-water streams that wind their way through the landscape. It’s these little touches of natural wonder that make our campground feel magical and unforgettable.
Whether you’re taking photos, exploring with the kids, or just sitting nearby with a coffee in hand, this connection to nature is what sets us apart.
Pet-Friendly (Because Dogs Are Family, Too)
We know that your dog is part of the family, and camping wouldn’t be the same without them. That’s why we welcome dogs, as long as they have a valid vaccination certificate and are kept on a leash while on the property.
With wide-open spaces, fresh air, and gentle trails, your dog will love the experience just as much as you do. It’s a great way for everyone in the family to enjoy the outdoors—paws included.
Spacious Campsites That Offer Real Privacy
We’ve all experienced those crowded campgrounds where your neighbor is just a few feet away. That’s not what you’ll find here. At Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée, our campsites are large, private, and thoughtfully spaced. You’ll have plenty of room for your tent or RV, a fire pit, and a picnic area—without the noise or lack of privacy that’s common in other places.
We believe that real camping means being able to enjoy nature on your own terms, with space to stretch out and relax.
Weekly Activities for All Ages
Camping is about more than just sitting by the fire—it’s about making memories. That’s why we host weekly activities designed to bring people together and add excitement to your stay. From themed weekends and outdoor games to arts and crafts for kids, movie nights, and music around the campfire, there’s always something to look forward to.
Whether you want to meet other campers or just entertain the family, our activities add a fun, community-driven touch to your visit.
A Place Where You Truly Belong
At Camping Domaine de la Frontière Enchantée, we’re more than just a place to park your RV or set up a tent—we’re a destination where people come to slow down, breathe deeply, and experience nature the way it was meant to be. With generous space, peaceful surroundings, and a commitment to making every guest feel welcome, we’ve created a campground that people return to year after year. Whether you’re a seasoned camper or trying it for the first time, we invite you to discover the charm, beauty, and simplicity of camping with us.
Come see why so many call us their home away from home.

all ages. Our magical cascading waterfall is a must-see, providing a serene and picturesque backdrop to your outdoor adventures. Enjoy a round of golf on our scenic 9-hole Par 3 course, which boasts beautiful views of the lake and waterfall. Dive into fun at our semi-Olympic swimming pool or explore our two stunning lakes, perfect for fishing, kayaking, and relaxation.
